======================================================================
INDIAN CONSTITUTION KNOWLEDGE BASE
======================================================================

Entry 1
Category: Constitution Basics
Concept: Constitution of India
Definition: The supreme law of India that came into effect on 26 January 1950.
Key Points:
- Fundamental constitutional concept.
- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.
Example:
Constitution of India is an important constitutional concept.
Related Concepts:
Fundamental Rights, Parliament, Judiciary, Federalism, Governance

Entry 2
Category: Constitution Basics
Concept: Constituent Assembly
Definition: The body that drafted the Constitution of India.
Key Points:
- Fundamental constitutional concept.
- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.
Example:
Constituent Assembly is an important constitutional concept.
Related Concepts:
Fundamental Rights, Parliament, Judiciary, Federalism, Governance

Entry 3
Category: Constitution Basics
Concept: Preamble
Definition: Introduces the ideals, objectives and philosophy of the Constitution.
Key Points:
- Fundamental constitutional concept.
- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.
Example:
Justice, Liberty, Equality and Fraternity are key ideals.
Related Concepts:
Fundamental Rights, Parliament, Judiciary, Federalism, Governance

Entry 4
Category: Constitution Basics
Concept: Sovereign

- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.

Entry 11
Category: Rights and Duties
Concept: Fundamental Rights
Definition: Basic rights guaranteed to all citizens under Part III.
Key Points:
- Fundamental constitutional concept.
- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.
Example:
Fundamental Rights is an important constitutional concept.
Related Concepts:

Entry 12
Category: Rights and Duties
Concept: Right to Equality

- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.
Example:
Article 14 guarantees equality before the law.

- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.
Example:
Citizens may approach the Supreme Court under Article 32.

Definition: The supreme law of India that came into effect on 26 January 1950.
Key Points:
- Fundamental constitutional concept.
- Frequently asked in UPSC, SSC and other competitive examinations.
- Important for understanding India's democratic system.
Example:
Constitution of India is an important constitutional concept.
Related Concepts:
Fundamental Rights, Parliament, Judiciary, Federalism, Governance
